# Art pending from integration round 3

Every row is an asset-check error of the full build of 24 October 2026 that only new image-generated art can fix.
None is invented here and none is baselined silently: each row's finding is in `docs/design/validation/asset_baseline.json` with a reason that names this file and the row.
When the art for a row ships, the finding disappears from the report and the build logs it as fixed; drop the row and regenerate the baseline entry with it.
The findings that code or data could fix were fixed in the builder instead (docs/design/stage2/integration_r3.md, section "Asset findings").

Totals: 792 rows (15 realm flags of the roster integration, 302 exact duplicates of the interface reskin, 297 focus icons over a tree's cap, 115 focus icons shared with a prerequisite, 38 focus icons over the game cap, 25 event pictures over the game cap).

## Realm flags (roster integration)

The 156 realms of the roster (tools/content/data/world/roster.json) have one heraldic design each in tools/content/data/world/heraldry.json. Their flag pictures come from tools/content/art/flag-jobs.mjs: sheets for the living, and one picture per flag for the dark realms whose sheet was rejected. All 156 realms have an accepted flag (tools/content/data/realm-flags-cells.json, accepted: true), which the builder stages (modules/art.mjs stageRealmFlags); no realm flies an old row flag any more.

## Generic portraits outside the roster plan

The portrait plan (tools/content/data/world/portrait_pools.json) covers the 1,033 roster characters. Six mechanic-recruited figures of the courts sit outside it: zin_aenor_golden_regency, zin_aenor_concord_speaker and zin_aenor_captains_regency (zin_aenor_court), zin_afrit_council figures (zin_afrit_council) and figures of zin_realm_characters. The chooser gives them one of the two legacy generics of their people, so six generics serve four to eleven characters each and exceed the pool cap (UNIQ_PORTRAIT_POOL). Each is accepted in the asset baseline with its reason.

Disposition: add these figures to the portrait plan with their own pool members when the owner decides whether they need pool portraits, or keep the legacy generics. Owner question candidate: which pool portraits the mechanic figures should wear.

## Super-event pictures and recordings

The super events of owner decision K52 (docs/specs/06_events/06_super_events.md) register one picture sprite and one song per moment under their final names (modules/super_events.mjs). No frame fallback stands in: until a picture ships the window shows the empty picture frame, and until a recording ships the moment plays no music.
Each picture row (SE) is an asset-check finding SPRITE_TEXTURE_MISSING accepted in docs/design/validation/asset_baseline.json with a reason naming its row; drop the row and its baseline entry when the picture ships.
The recording rows (SA) are not asset-check findings: the check does not read the mod's own music/*.asset files (tools/content/lib/loaded_assets.mjs skips them), so a missing song file is not reported; the rows are the record until the audio specialist delivers.
Briefs: docs/plans/super_events/art_briefs.json and docs/plans/super_events/audio_briefs.json.
